Potential algorithm:
For each candidate length n in a list [3,4,5,6,7,8,10,12,16,24,32,64,... maybe parent length]:
  - Initialize multiple p:
     * uniform
     * geometric ratios r in logspace (0.1..0.95) and reversed
     * random Dirichlet
     * low-rank sinusoidal: p=softplus(base + a cos + b cos2 + c cos3) normalized.
  - Evaluate exact score, keep best.
  - Run local optimization from best and a few random starts:
      Optimize unconstrained y; p=exp(y)/sum.
      Objective smooth F(y)=τ logsumexp(conv(p,p)/τ) (since sum p=1). Minimize F. Use L-BFGS-B? No bounds needed for y. scipy.optimize.minimize(method='L-BFGS-B', jac=grad) or 'BFGS'. n small, BFGS OK. Or implement gradient descent with line search.
      Compute exact score after each or at end.
  - Keep global best across n.
Return best list.

Then grad_p_i = 2 ∑_k w_k p_{k-i} I[0≤k-i<n] = 2 ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} w_{i+j} p_j (where i+j ≤2n-2). This is cross-correlation of w (length 2n-1) and p (length n): grad_p_i = 2 * corr_i, where corr_i = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} w_{i+j} p_j.

Algorithm:
- p normalized sum1.
- For iteration:
   q = conv(p,p) (FFT? np.convolve)
   compute F = tau*logsumexp(q/tau)
   w = softmax(q/tau)
   corr = np.correlate(w, p, 'full')[:n]
   grad = 2*corr
   gnorm = grad / (max(grad)+eps)
   try step sizes [eta, eta/2,...] where p_trial = p * exp(-eta*gnorm); normalize; compute F_trial = tau logsum(conv(p_trial,trial)/tau). Accept if F_trial < F + c*eta*? For mirror descent, just accept if F_trial < F (monotone). If none, reduce eta and accept first or break.
- Update exact score periodically.

Param family details:
N = n-1 to include endpoints. theta0 trend in [-3,3], theta1..theta5 in [-2,2]. p_i = exp(theta0*x + sum). Normalize. We can use scipy.optimize.minimize on θ with smooth objective for length n=3809 (fft/direct conv). Since d small, BFGS gradient wrt θ can be computed by chain rule: grad_p as before, grad_x = p * d(log p)/dθ. We can implement. This is promising! It optimizes a smooth (nonlinear) family that can be asymmetric and oscillatory. We can run multi-start BFGS in θ-space for the full length n. Each objective/gradient costs conv+correlate O(n^2), but number of calls maybe few hundred; okay.

Since θ dimension ~6, we can also use Nelder-Mead or random search. The family may be rich enough to reach target. Let's implement.

Function param_seq(theta, n):
  N = n-1
  x = i/N (if n>1)
  z = theta[0]*x
  for m=1..M: z += theta[1+2m-1]*cos(2π m i/N) + theta[2+2m-1]*sin(...)
  p = exp(z - z.max()) / sum.
Param derivative dz/dtheta: [x, cos/sin list]. We can compute grad_y = p * (grad_p - p.grad_p) for p variable (as if p optimized). Actually log p = z - log S. d log p_i /dθ = dz_i/dθ - ∑ p_j dz_j/dθ. Then grad_theta = ∑_i grad_p_i * p_i * dlogp_i/dθ? More directly, F as function of p; dF/dθ = ∑_i grad_p_i * dp_i/dθ. dp_i/dθ = p_i (dlogp_i/dθ). So grad_theta = (p*grad_p) @ Dlog, where Dlog is n x d. We can compute:
   r = p*grad_p  (length n)
   for each theta component φ: grad_theta[d] = ∑ r_i * dlogp_i/dθ_d
   dlogp_i = dz_i/dθ_d - mean_weighted(dz) = dz_i - p.Dz.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _clean(seq):
   a=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   return a

def _score(seq):
   try: return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in seq]))
   except: return np.inf
```
But converting large list each time overhead; evaluator accepts list; can pass seq.tolist(). We'll use.

Normalize:
```python
def _normalize(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel(); a=np.maximum(a,0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
   a=a/s
   return np.clip(a,0,1000)
```

Smooth helper:
```python
def _convolution(p): return np.convolve(p,p)

def _smooth_obj_grad_p(p, tau=None):
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return np.inf, None
    if tau is None: tau = max(1e-12, 1.0/n)
    q=np.convolve(p,p)
    # q nonnegative but clip tiny
    q=np.where(q>0, q, 0.0) maybe? If all zero return.
    m=float(q.max())
    if m <= 0: return 0.0, np.zeros(n)
    z=(q - m)/tau
    # to avoid under/over, z<=0
    ez=np.exp(z)
    s=ez.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite: ez = np.zeros_like(q); ez[np.argmax(z)] = 1; s=1
    w=ez/s
    F = n*(m + tau*math.log(s))  # scaled n*F
    corr = np.correlate(w, p, mode='full')[:n]
    grad = 2.0*corr
    return F, grad
```
But if q has values much less than m, z very negative, exp underflows to 0; s maybe small but okay. If all but argmax underflow, w one-hot at max. Good.

For param θ, need compute p and features. We can precompute X once per n:
```python
def _make_features(n, modes=8):
   if n==1: X=np.ones((1,1)); return X
   i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   N=float(n-1)
   X=np.empty((n,1+2*modes))
   X[:,0]=i/N
   for m in range(1,modes+1):
      angle=2*math.pi*m*i/N
      X[:,1+2*(m-1)] = np.cos(angle)
      X[:,2+2*(m-1)] = np.sin(angle)
   return X
```
For n=3809, X 3809x17 ~65k floats? 3809*17=64753, 0.5MB. Good.

p_from_theta:
```python
def _theta_p(theta, X):
   z = X @ theta
   z -= z.max()
   p = np.exp(z)
   p /= p.sum()
   return p
```
Gradient theta:
```python
def _theta_obj_grad(theta, X, tau=None):
   p=_theta_p(theta,X)
   F,gp = _smooth_obj_grad_p(p,tau)
   r = p*gp
   Xp = X.T @ p
   Xr = X.T @ r
   grad_theta = Xr - (r.sum())*Xp
   # F already scaled n
   return F, grad_theta
```

No, we subtract max, not mean; adding same constant to all z leaves p unchanged if z_i all shifted equally. Mirror descent function:
```python
def _mirror_descent(p, iterations, deadline, best_cb):
    n=p.size
    tau=1.0/n
    eta=0.8
    stall=0
    last_eval=0
    for it in range(iterations):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       F,gp = _smooth_obj_grad_p(p,tau)
       gmax = gp.max()
       if not finite or gmax<=0: break
       gnorm = gp/gmax
       improved=False
       for step in [eta, eta/2, eta/4, eta/8]:
           p_try = p * np.exp(-step*gnorm)
           s=p_try.sum()
           if s<=0 or not finite: continue
           p_try /= s
           F_try,_ = _smooth_obj_grad_p(p_try,tau)
           if F_try < F - 1e-12:
               p=p_try; improved=True; break
       if improved:
           stall=0
           # evaluate exact every 50
       else:
           stall +=1
           eta = max(0.05, eta*0.8)
           if stall>50:
               # random kick
               noise = 0.05*rng.normal(size=n)
               p = p * np.exp(noise); p/=p.sum()
               stall=0; eta=0.5
       if (it+1)%25==0:
           score=exact p; best_cb(p,score)
```
This line search calls smooth twice (F and F_try) per accepted step, plus rejected. Could be costly. We can skip line search and use fixed eta with occasional exact. But monotone helps. To reduce cost, use F from current, and for p_try compute only F_try (conv+correlate). If rejected several, cost. We'll limit: try 2 sizes [eta, eta/2]. If none, reduce eta and accept without check? Accepting may increase F but randomization. Use:
   p_try = ...; F_try,_ = ...; if F_try < F: accept else eta=eta*0.7; p=p_best? Maybe keep best smooth.
Better: do one trial; if F_trial < F, accept; else reduce eta. No multiple trials. This is cheap. But if eta too large, may never accept. Use eta adaptation.

Upsampling small p to full N:
```python
def _upsample(p, N):
   L=p.size
   if L==N: return p.copy()
   x_old=np.arange(L); y_old=p
   x_new=np.linspace(0,L-1,N)
   pN=np.interp(x_new, x_old, y_old)
   return _normalize(pN)
```
This treats sequence values linearly; sum may not 1. Good.

Small optimizer using softmax y:
```python
def _optimize_small_y(L, p0, maxfun=30):
   y=np.log(p0+1e-300) - np.mean(np.log(p0+1e-300))
   def fun_grad(y):
      p=np.exp(y); p/=p.sum()
      F,gp=_smooth_obj_grad_p(p,tau=1.0/L)
      gdot = float(np.dot(p,gp))
      grad_y = p*(gp-gdot)
      return F, grad_y
   res=minimize(fun_grad, y, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxfun':maxfun})
   p=np.exp(res.x); p/=p.sum()
   return p
```
This optimizes all-positive simplex. It may not find zeros but small n likely positive. For each L, starts: uniform, random Dirichlet 8, parametric. BFGS maxfun=20. Very cheap. Consider exact.

But if target requires zeros/boundaries, softmax approximates with large y; BFGS bounds maybe allow y in [-30,30]? L-BFGS-B default unbounded; we can set bounds (-40,40). Good.

Param θ search implementation details:
```python
def _theta_starts(d, rng, n_deterministic=8):
   inits=[]
   inits.append(np.zeros(d))
   # trends
   for t in [-3,-2,-1,1,2,3]:
      th=np.zeros(d); th[0]=t; inits.append(th)
   # mode 1 cos/sin
   for j in [1,2]: for val in [-2,-1,1,2]: th=zeros; th[j]=val
   # random
   for _ in range(12):
      th = rng.normal(size=d)
      th[0]*=1.5
      th[1:]*=1.0
      inits.append(th)
   return inits
```

```python
def _optimize_theta(N, modes, max_bfgs, maxfun, deadline, rng):
   X = make_features(N,modes)
   d=X.shape[1]
   inits = ...
   scored = []
   for th in inits:
      p=theta_p(th,X); sc=score(p); scored.append((sc,th))
   scored.sort(key first)
   best_theas=[]
   for sc,th in scored[:max_bfgs]:
      if time>deadline: break
      bounds = [(-6,6)] + [(-4,4)]*(d-1)
      def fg(th):
         return _theta_obj_grad(th,X, tau=0.5/N)
      try: res=minimize(fg, th, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxfun':maxfun,'gtol':1e-4})
         th2=res.x
      except: th2=th
      p=theta_p(th2,X)
      sc=score(p)
      consider(p,N)
      best_theas.append((sc,th2))
   return best_theas
```

```python
def _corr_fft(w, p):
   n=p.size
   if n==1: return w.copy()? corr0 = w0*p0.
   L=1
   while L < 3*n-2: L <<=1
   W=np.fft.rfft(w, L)
   R=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], L)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(W*R, L)[:3*n-2]
   corr=conv[:n][::-1]
   # due to numerical round, small imaginary? irfft real. clip?
   return corr
```

```python
def _smooth_F(p,tau):
   n=p.size; q=np.convolve(p,p); m=q.max(); if m<=0 return 0; z=(q-m)/tau; ez=np.exp(z); s=ez.sum(); return n*(m+tau*log(s))
```

Let's draft code mentally.

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    total = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = start_time + max(1.0, total - 40.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_score = np.inf
    best_seq = None

    def as_list(seq): return [float(x) for x in seq]
    def consider(seq, score=None):
       nonlocal best_score, best_seq
       a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if a.size == 0: return np.inf
       a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
       a = np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
       if score is None:
           try: score = float(evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()))
           except: score = np.inf
       if not np.isfinite(score): return score
       if score < best_score:
           best_score = score
           best_seq = a.copy()
       return score
```

`np.nan_to_num` with posinf=1000 etc available? Yes.

Helper normalize:
```
    def normalize(a):
       a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       a=np.nan_to_num(a,...); a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
       s=a.sum()
       if s<=1e-300 or not finite: return np.ones_like(a)/max(1,a.size)
       a=a/s
       return np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
```

Smooth helpers as nested (use math.log):
```
    def smooth_F_and_grad(p, tau):
       n=p.size
       q=np.convolve(p,p)
       q = np.where(q > 0.0, q, 0.0)  # avoid tiny negatives
       m=float(q.max())
       if m <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf, np.zeros(n)
       z=(q-m)/tau
       ez=np.exp(z)
       s=ez.sum()
       if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
          ez = np.zeros_like(q); ez[int(np.argmax(z))] = 1.0; s=1.0
       w=ez/s
       F = n*(m + tau*math.log(s))
       if n > 256:
          corr = corr_ff(w,p)
       else:
          full = np.correlate(w,p,'full')
          corr = full[n-1:2*n-1]
       corr = np.maximum(corr,0.0)
       grad = 2.0*corr
       return F, grad
```
`corr_ff` nested needs L. Use `next_power_two` maybe.
```
    def corr_fft(w,p):
       n=p.size
       need=3*n-2
       L=1
       while L < need: L <<=1
       W=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
       R=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],L)
       conv=np.fft.irfft(W*R,L)
       corr=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
       return corr
```
Memory for L=16384, fine. For n small >256, L maybe 2048/4096, fine.

Smooth F only:
```
    def smooth_F(p,tau):
       n=p.size; q=np.convolve(p,p); q=np.where(q>0,q,0); m=max(q); ... same without corr
```
Could factor q, softmax. To avoid duplicate code, but fine.

Small optimizer:
```
    def small_optimize(L,p0,maxfun=20):
       p0=normalize(p0); p0=np.maximum(p0,1e-300)
       y=np.log(p0); y-=y.mean()
       tau=0.2/L
       bounds=[(-40,40)]*L
       def fg(y):
          yy=y-y.max()
          p=np.exp(yy); p/=p.sum()
          F,gp=smooth_F_and_grad(p,tau)
          if not finite: return 1e6, np.zeros(L)
          grad = p*(gp - float(np.dot(p,gp)))
          return F, grad
       try:
          res=minimize(fg, y, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxfun':maxfun,'gtol':1e-4,'maxiter':maxfun})
          yy=res.x-res.x.max(); p=np.exp(yy); p/=p.sum()
       except Exception:
          p=p0
       return p
```
L-BFGS-B maxiter >=? options valid: 'maxiter', 'maxfun', 'disp', 'gtol', 'ftol', 'maxcor'. Good.

Param features and θ:
```
    def make_features(n,modes):
       if n <=1: return np.ones((1,1))
       i=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
       denom=float(n-1)
       X=np.empty((n,1+2*modes), dtype=np.float64)
       X[:,0]=i/denom
       for m in range(1,modes+1):
          angle=2.0*math.pi*m*i/denom
          col=1+2*(m-1)
          X[:,col]=np.cos(angle)
          X[:,col+1]=np.sin(angle)
       return X
    def theta_p(theta,X):
       z=X @ theta
       z=z-z.max()
       p=np.exp(z)
       s=p.sum()
       if s<=0 or not finite: p=np.ones_like(z)/z.size
       else: p/=s
       return p
    def theta_obj_grad(theta,X,tau):
       p=theta_p(theta,X)
       F,gp=smooth_F_and_grad(p,tau)
       if not finite: return 1e6, np.zeros_like(theta)
       r=p*gp
       Xp=X.T @ p
       Xr=X.T @ r
       grad = Xr - float(np.sum(r))*Xp
       # To reduce scale invariance, maybe subtract mean? not necessary.
       return F, grad
```
Bounds θ:
```
    def theta_bounds(d):
       b=[(-8,8)]+ [(-6,6)]*(d-1)
       return b
```
Starts:
```
    def theta_inits(d, rng):
       inits=[np.zeros(d)]
       for t in (-3,-2,-1,1,2,3):
          th=np.zeros(d); th[0]=t; inits.append(th)
       for idx in (1,2):
          for val in (-2,-1,1,2):
             th=np.zeros(d); th[idx]=val; inits.append(th)
       for _ in range(24):
          th=rng.normal(size=d)
          th[0]*=2.0
          th[1:]*=1.2
          inits.append(th)
       return inits
```
NS > d? idx 1,2 valid if d>=3 (modes≥1). Good.

`optimize_theta(N,modes,max_bfgs,maxfun)`:
```
    def optimize_theta(N,modes,max_bfgs,maxfun):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: return []
       X=make_features(N,modes)
       d=X.shape[1]
       inits=theta_inits(d,rng)
       scored=[]
       for th in inits:
          if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
          p=theta_p(th,X)
          sc=consider(p)
          scored.append((sc,th))
       scored.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])
       results=[]
       tau=0.5/N
       bounds=theta_bounds(d)
       for sc,th in scored:
          if len(results)>=max_bfgs or time.monotonic()>deadline: break
          if not np.isfinite(sc) and sc>=best_score: continue
          def fg(th): return theta_obj_grad(th,X,tau)
          try:
             res=minimize(fg, th, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxfun':maxfun,'gtol':1e-4})
             th2=np.asarray(res.x)
          except Exception:
             th2=th
          p=theta_p(th2,X)
          sc2=consider(p)
          results.append((sc2,th2,p))
       return results
```
Note consider computes exact but `scored` sorted includes best possibly global. Good.

Upsample:
```
    def upsample(a,N):
       a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
       if a.size==N: return a.copy()
       if a.size==1: out=np.full(N,a.item())
       elif N==1: out=a[:1].copy()
       else:
          x_new=np.linspace(0,a.size-1,N)
          out=np.interp(x_new, np.arange(a.size,a.size,dtype=float), a)
       return normalize(out)
```
If N=1? Parent n could 1? small? Up handling.

```
             parent=normalize(par); parent_n=par.size
    except Exception: pass
    if parent is None:
       parent=np.ones(512); parent_n=512
```

Mirror function:
```
    def mirror_descent(p0, sub_deadline):
       nonlocal best_score,best_seq
       p=normalize(p0)
       n=p.size
       tau=0.15/n
       local_best=p.copy(); local_score=consider(p) if? We can compute exact once:
       s0=consider(p)
       if np.isfinite(s0): local_score=s0; local_best=p.copy()
       else: local_score=inf
       eta=0.7
       stall=0
       it=0
       smooth_best_F = smooth_F(p,tau)
       while time.monotonic() < sub_deadline:
          it +=1
          F,gp=smooth_F_and_grad(p,tau)
          if not finite: break
          gmax=float(gp.max())
          if not finite or gmax <= 0: break
          gnorm=gp/gmax
          accepted=False
          e=eta
          while e >= 0.02:
             p_try = p * np.exp(-e*gnorm)
             s=p_try.sum()
             if not finite or s <=0: break
             p_try=p_try/s
             F_try=smooth_F(p_try,tau)
             if np.isfinite(F_try) and F_try < F - 1e-13:
                p=p_try; F=F_try; accepted=True; break
             e*=0.6
          if accepted:
             stall=0
             eta=min(2.0, eta*1.05)
             # every 25 exact
             if it % 25 ==0:
                sc=consider(p)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_score:
                   local_score=sc; local_best=p.copy()
          else:
             stall +=1
             eta=max(0.02, eta*0.7)
             if stall > 40:
                # kick from local_best
                noise = 0.08*rng.normal(size=n)
                p = local_best * np.exp(noise)
                p=np.clip(p,0,None); s=p.sum()
                if s>0: p/=s
                F, _ = smooth_F_and_grad(p,tau)
                stall=0; eta=0.7
                if it % 25 ==0: consider(p)
       # final exact
       sc=consider(p)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_score: ...
```
But `consider(p)` inside compute exact, but we also need local. We can compute local score by calling a helper that returns score. Fine.

Cost: `smooth_F_and_grad` at start of every iteration and after kick. In rejected loop, multiple `smooth_F` trials. If eta never accepts, many trials. Limit: max 4 trials (e loop). Good.

If p_try smooth_F not finite, break.

If best_seq has zeros from small support, upsample may have zeros? np.interp between zeros can produce zero segments.
```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = start_time + max(1.0, total_budget - 60.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_score = np.inf
    best_seq = None

    def _clean_arr(x):
        a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        return np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    def _consider(seq, score=None):
        nonlocal best_score, best_seq
        a = _clean_arr(seq)
        if a.size == 0:
            return np.inf
        if score is None:
            try:
                score = float(evaluate_sequence(a.tolist()))
            except Exception:
                score = np.inf
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            return score
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_seq = a.copy()
        return score

    def _normalize(a):
        a = _clean_arr(a)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            n = max(1, a.size)
            return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        a = a / s
        return a

    def _softmax_from_y(y):
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
        y = y - float(y.max())
        p = np.exp(y)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            p = np.ones_like(y, dtype=np.float64)
            s = float(p.size)
        return p / s

    def _corr_fft(w, p):
        n = p.size
        if n <= 1:
            return w[:1].copy()
        need = 3 * n - 2
        L = 1
        while L < need:
            L <<= 1
        W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
        R = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], L)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(W * R, L)
        return conv[n - 1 : 2 * n - 1]

    def _smooth_F_and_gradp(p, tau):
        n = p.size
        if n <= 0:
            return np.inf, None
        if n <= 1:
            return n * float(p[0]), np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        q = np.convolve(p, p)
        q = np.where(q > 0.0, q, 0.0)
        m = float(q.max())
        if m <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
            return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        z = (q - m) / tau
        ez = np.exp(z)
        s = float(ez.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            ez = np.zeros_like(q)
            ez[int(np.argmax(z))] = 1.0
            s = 1.0
        w = ez / s
        F = n * (m + tau * math.log(s))
        if n > 256:
            corr = _corr_fft(w, p)
        else:
            full = np.correlate(w, p, mode="full")
            corr = full[n - 1 : 2 * n - 1]
        corr = np.where(np.isfinite(corr), corr, 0.0)
        corr = np.maximum(corr, 0.0)
        gradp = 2.0 * corr
        return F, gradp

    def _smooth_F_only(p, tau):
        n = p.size
        if n <= 0:
            return np.inf
        if n <= 1:
            return n * float(p[0])
        q = np.convolve(p, p)
        q = np.where(q > 0.0, q, 0.0)
        m = float(q.max())
        if m <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
            return 0.0
        z = (q - m) / tau
        ez = np.exp(z)
        s = float(ez.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return n * m
        return n * (m + tau * math.log(s))

    def _small_optimizer(L, p0, max_iter=25):
        p0 = _normalize(p0)
        p0 = np.maximum(p0, 1e-300)
        y0 = np.log(p0)
        y0 -= float(y0.mean())
        tau = max(1e-15, 0.25 / max(1, L))
        bounds = [(-40.0, 40.0)] * max(1, L)

        def fg(y):
            p = _softmax_from_y(y)
            F, gp = _smooth_F_and_gradp(p, tau)
            if not np.isfinite(F):
                return 1e6, np.zeros(max(1, L), dtype=np.float64)
            dot = float(np.dot(p, gp))
            gy = p * (gp - dot)
            return F, gy

        try:
            res = minimize(
                fg,
                y0,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": max_iter, "gtol": 1e-5},
            )
            p = _softmax_from_y(res.x)
        except Exception:
            p = p0
        return _normalize(p)

    def _make_features(n, modes=8):
        if n <= 1:
            return np.ones((max(1, n), 1), dtype=np.float64)
        i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        denom = float(n - 1)
        X = np.empty((n, 1 + 2 * max(0, modes)), dtype=np.float64)
        X[:, 0] = i / denom
        for m in range(1, max(1, modes) + 1):
            ang = 2.0 * math.pi * m * i / denom
            c0 = 1 + 2 * (m - 1)
            X[:, c0] = np.cos(ang)
            X[:, c0 + 1] = np.sin(ang)
        return X

    def _theta_to_p(theta, X):
        theta = _clean_arr(theta)
        z = X @ theta
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
        p = np.exp(z - float(z.max()))
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            p = np.ones_like(z, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            p = p / s
        return p

    def _theta_obj_grad(theta, X, tau):
        p = _theta_to_p(theta, X)
        F, gp = _smooth_F_and_gradp(p, tau)
        if not np.isfinite(F):
            return 1e6, np.zeros_like(theta, dtype=np.float64)
        r = p * gp
        Xp = X.T @ p
        Xr = X.T @ r
        grad = Xr - float(np.sum(r)) * Xp
        return F, grad

    def _theta_inits(d, rng_, num_random=24):
        inits = [np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)]
        if d > 0:
            for t in (-3.0, -2.0, -1.0, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
                th = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
                th[0] = t
                inits.append(th)
        if d >= 3:
            for idx in (1, 2):
                for val in (-2.0, -1.0, 1.0, 2.0):
                    th = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
                    th[idx] = val
                    inits.append(th)
        if d >= 5:
            for idx in (3, 4):
                for val in (-1.5, 1.5):
                    th = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
                    th[idx] = val
                    inits.append(th)
        for _ in range(max(0, int(num_random))):
            th = rng_.normal(size=d)
            if th.size > 0:
                th[0] *= 2.0
            if th.size > 1:
                th[1:] *= 1.25
            inits.append(th)
        return inits

    def _theta_bounds(d):
        if d <= 0:
            return []
        return [(-6.0, 6.0)] + [(-5.0, 5.0)] * max(0, d - 1)

    def _optimize_theta(N, modes, max_bfgs=8, max_iter=70, num_random=24, time_reserve=30.0):
        if N <= 0:
            return []
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - max(1.0, time_reserve):
            return []
        X = _make_features(N, modes)
        d = X.shape[1]
        inits = _theta_inits(d, rng, num_random)
        scored = []
        for th in inits:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - max(1.0, time_reserve):
                break
            p = _theta_to_p(th, X)
            sc = _consider(p)
            scored.append((sc, th))
        scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0] if np.isfinite(z[0]) else np.inf)

        tau = max(1e-15, 0.5 / max(1, N))
        bounds = _theta_bounds(d)
        results = []
        for sc, th in scored:
            if len(results) >= max_bfgs:
                break
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - max(1.0, time_reserve):
                break
            if not np.isfinite(sc) and sc >= best_score:
                continue

            def fg(th):
                return _theta_obj_grad(th, X, tau)

            try:
                res = minimize(
                    fg,
                    th,
                    jac=True,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options={"maxiter": max_iter, "gtol": 1e-5},
                )
                th2 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            except Exception:
                th2 = th
            p = _theta_to_p(th2, X)
            sc2 = _consider(p)
            results.append((sc2, th2, p))
        return results

    def _upsample(a, N):
        a = _clean_arr(a)
        a = _normalize(a)
        if N <= 0:
            return a.copy()
        L = a.size
        if L == N:
            return a.copy()
        if L <= 1:
            out = np.full(max(1, N), float(a[0]) if a.size else 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
            return _normalize(out)
        if N <= 1:
            return a[:1].copy()
        x_old = np.arange(L, dtype=np.float64)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, float(L - 1), max(1, N))
        out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
        out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
        return _normalize(out)

    # Use the supplied parent construction if it is available.
    parent = None
    parent_n = 512
    try:
        par = _clean_arr(height_sequence_1)
        if par.size > 0 and float(par.sum()) > 0.0:
            parent = _normalize(par)
            parent_n = par.size
    except Exception:
        parent = None
        parent_n = 512

    if parent is None:
        parent = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)
        parent_n = 512
        parent = parent / float(parent.sum())

    _consider(parent)

    if parent_n < 1:
        parent_n = 1
        parent = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        _consider(parent)

    # Keep all expensive full-length work within a sane memory/time window.
    N_full = int(parent_n)
    if N_full > 8192:
        N_full = 4096

    # 1) Search compact lengths directly.
    small_lengths = [3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 10, 12, 16, 24, 32, 48, 64, 96, 128]
    for L in small_lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
            break
        idx = np.arange(L, dtype=np.float64)
        starts = [np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)]
        for a in (1.5, -1.5, 2.5, -2.5, 4.0, -4.0):
            v = np.maximum((idx + 1.0) ** float(a), 1e-12)
            starts.append(v)
        for _ in range(4 if L <= 16 else 6):
            v = rng.normal(size=L)
            p = _softmax_from_y(v)
            starts.append(p)

        max_iter = 30 if L <= 12 else (20 if L <= 32 else 12)
        for p0 in starts:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
                break
            p = _small_optimizer(L, p0, max_iter=max_iter)
            _consider(p)

    # 2) Learn a low-dimensional positive density shape on a coarse grid.
    N_coarse = int(min(512, max(128, N_full // 4))) if N_full > 512 else max(64, N_full)
    N_coarse = max(1, N_coarse)
    coarse_results = _optimize_theta(
        N=N_coarse,
        modes=8,
        max_bfgs=8,
        max_iter=60,
        num_random=28,
        time_reserve=60.0,
    )

    # 3) Evaluate/refine the same shape family at the working full length.
    full_theta_results = []
    if N_full >= 1 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0:
        X_full = _make_features(N_full, modes=8)
        d_full = X_full.shape[1]
        bounds_full = _theta_bounds(d_full)
        tau_full = max(1e-15, 0.5 / max(1, N_full))

        seed_thetas = []
        for _, th, _ in coarse_results[:4]:
            seed_thetas.append(th)

        def fg_full(theta):
            return _theta_obj_grad(theta, X_full, tau_full)

        for k, th0 in enumerate(seed_thetas):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
                break
            p0 = _theta_to_p(th0, X_full)
            _consider(p0)
            full_theta_results.append((np.inf, th0, p0))
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    fg_full,
                    th0,
                    jac=True,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds_full,
                    options={"maxiter": 45 - 5 * k, "gtol": 1e-5},
                )
                p = _theta_to_p(res.x, X_full)
                sc = _consider(p)
                full_theta_results.append((sc, np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), p))
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Also run a full-length parameter search from additional random inits.
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
            for th0 in _theta_inits(d_full, rng, num_random=36)[:12]:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 100.0:
                    break
                p0 = _theta_to_p(th0, X_full)
                sc = _consider(p0)
                full_theta_results.append((sc, th0, p0))
                try:
                    res = minimize(
                        fg_full,
                        th0,
                        jac=True,
                        method="L-BFGS-B",
                        bounds=bounds_full,
                        options={"maxiter": 35, "gtol": 1e-5},
                    )
                    p = _theta_to_p(res.x, X_full)
                    sc2 = _consider(p)
                    full_theta_results.append((sc2, np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), p))
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # 4) Full-length multiplicative gradient polishing.
    def _mirror_descent(p0, sub_deadline):
        nonlocal best_score, best_seq
        p = _upsample(p0, N_full)
        p = np.maximum(p, 1e-12)
        p = _normalize(p)
        n = p.size
        tau = max(1e-15, 0.15 / max(1, n))

        local_score = _consider(p)
        local_best = p.copy()
        if not np.isfinite(local_score):
            local_score = np.inf

        eta = 0.7
        stall = 0
        it = 0
        last_exact = time.monotonic()

        while time.monotonic() < sub_deadline:
            it += 1
            F, gp = _smooth_F_and_gradp(p, tau)
            if gp is None or not np.isfinite(F):
                break
            gmax = float(gp.max())
            if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 0.0:
                break
            gnorm = gp / gmax

            accepted = False
            e = eta
            while e >= 0.02:
                p_try = p * np.exp(-e * gnorm)
                p_try = np.where(np.isfinite(p_try), p_try, 0.0)
                s = float(p_try.sum())
                if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                    break
                p_try = p_try / s
                F_try = _smooth_F_only(p_try, tau)
                if np.isfinite(F_try) and F_try < F - 1e-13:
                    p = p_try
                    F = F_try
                    accepted = True
                    break
                e *= 0.6

            if accepted:
                stall = 0
                eta = min(1.8, eta * 1.05)
            else:
                stall += 1
                eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.7)
                if stall > 35:
                    base = np.maximum(local_best, 1e-12)
                    noise = 0.10 * rng.normal(size=n)
                    p = base * np.exp(noise)
                    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
                    s = float(p.sum())
                    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
                        p = p / s
                    else:
                        p = local_best.copy()
                    stall = 0
                    eta = 0.7

            if it % 20 == 0 or (time.monotonic() - last_exact) > 3.0:
                sc = _consider(p)
                last_exact = time.monotonic()
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_score:
                    local_score = sc
                    local_best = p.copy()

        sc = _consider(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_score:
            local_score = sc
            local_best = p.copy()
        return local_best

    mirror_starts = []
    if best_seq is not None:
        mirror_starts.append(best_seq)
    mirror_starts.append(parent)
    for sc, th, p in coarse_results[:2]:
        if p is not None and p.size > 0:
            mirror_starts.append(p)
    for sc, th, p in full_theta_results[:4]:
        if p is not None and p.size == N_full:
            mirror_starts.append(p)

    # Add a couple of random full-length starts if there is ample time left.
    if N_full <= 8192 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 300.0:
        for _ in range(2):
            v = rng.normal(size=N_full)
            p = _softmax_from_y(v)
            mirror_starts.append(p)

    # Remove exact duplicates by identity is unnecessary; short mirror runs are cheap.
    caps = [600.0, 300.0, 150.0, 70.0, 35.0]
    for k, p0 in enumerate(mirror_starts):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 60.0:
            break
        cap = caps[k] if k < len(caps) else 30.0
        remaining = deadline - 60.0 - time.monotonic()
        dur = min(float(cap), float(remaining))
        if dur <= 25.0:
            break
        _mirror_descent(p0, time.monotonic() + dur)

    # Final guard: return a valid non-empty finite list.
    final_seq = best_seq
    if final_seq is None or final_seq.size == 0:
        final_seq = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)
        final_seq /= float(final_seq.sum())
        _consider(final_seq)
        final_seq = best_seq if best_seq is not None else final_seq

    final_seq = _clean_arr(final_seq)
    if final_seq.size == 0:
        final_seq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    return [float(x) for x in final_seq]
```
